BUSINESS REVIEW                                                          業務回顧
We are a jewelry company in China with a nationwide                      我們是一家中國珠寶公司，擁有全國的銷售網絡及
sales network and highly recognized brand. Leveraging our                高度認可的品牌。依託我們全面的線下門店網絡與
comprehensive offline store network and online sales channels,           線上銷售渠道，我們為終端消費者提供各種珠寶產
we provide end consumers with a variety of jewelry products,             品，包括黃金珠寶、鑽石鑲嵌珠寶及其他產品。
including gold jewelry, diamond-set jewelry and others.


Our business model integrates the development and design,                我們的業務模式集珠寶產品的開發設計、採購供
procurement and supply, franchising and brand operation of               應、加盟、品牌運營為一體，連接產業價值鏈各
jewelry products, linking the various streams in the industry value      環節。於報告期間，我們的收入主要來自(i)加盟模
chain. During the reporting period, our revenue was primarily            式；(ii)線上銷售；及(iii)線下自營店。
derived from (i) franchise model; (ii) online sales; and (iii) offline
self-operated stores.

gold prices, end-market demand and industry policy updates, the          新等因素影響，本集團收入為約人民幣5,834.3百
Group’s recorded revenue of approximately RMB5,834.3 million,            萬元，較上年同期約人民幣5,718.2百萬元增加約

million for the corresponding period last year. The Group’s gross        1,478.8百萬元增加約人民幣125.4百萬元，增幅約

The Company is a jewelry company in China with nationwide                本公司是一家中國珠寶公司，擁有全國的銷售網絡

business model integrates the development and design,                    應、加盟、品牌運營為一體，連接產業價值鏈各

chain. During the reporting period, our revenue was primarily

self-operated stores.

shareholders shall report and pay the relevant taxes payable by
themselves. Any dividend received in respect of H Shares of the
Company which have been continuously held by a domestic
enterprise shareholder for 12 months shall be exempted from
enterprise income tax.

RELATIONSHIP WITH STAKEHOLDERS                                       與利益相關者的關系
The Group recognises that the employees, customers and               本集團深知僱員、客戶及供應商對企業持續發展至
suppliers are keys to corporate sustainable development and are      關重要並熱衷發展與持份者的長期關係。本公司非
keen on developing long-term relationships with stakeholders.        常注重人力資本並致力營造令僱員可全面開發其潛
The Company places significant emphasis on human capital             能並協助彼等實現個人及專業發展的環境。本公司
and strives to foster an environment in which the employees          提供公平安全的工作場所，提倡員工多元化發展，
can develop their full potential and to assist their personal and    並根據其成績及表現提供具競爭力的薪酬及福利以
professional growth. The Company provides a fair and safe            及職業發展機會。本集團亦持續努力為僱員提供完
workplace, promoting diversity to its staff, providing competitive   備的培訓及發展資源，令彼等能夠緊跟市場及行業
remuneration and benefits and career development opportunities       最新發展，同時改善其表現及其在職務上的自我實
based on their merits and performance. The Group also puts on-       現。本公司了解與客戶維持良好關係的重要性。本
going efforts to provide adequate training and development           集團已制定程序處理客戶投訴及進行客戶滿意度調
resources to the employees so that they can keep abreast of the      查，以確保客戶投訴得以快速及時處理。本集團亦
latest development of the market and the industry and, at the        致力與供應商建立長遠業務夥伴關係，以確保材料
same time, improve their performance and self-fulfillment in         的穩定供應。本集團透過不斷與供應商及承包商進
their positions. The Company understands that it is important        行積極有效的溝通加強與彼等的業務合作關係以確
to maintain good relationship with customers. The Group has          保質量及交付。
established procedures in place for handling customers’ complaints
and customer satisfaction surveys in order to ensure customers’
complaints are dealt with in a prompt and timely manner. The

     PRINCIPAL RISKS AND UNCERTAINTIES                                        主要風險與不確定性
     We believe that there are certain risks and uncertainties involved       我們相信我們的營運涉及若干風險及不確定因素，
     in our operations, some of which are beyond our control. We have         其中部分並非我們所能控制。我們已將該等風險及
     categorised these risks and uncertainties into the followings:           不確定因素分類為：


     (i)      We may not be able to effectively meet consumer demand.         (i)     我們可能無法有效滿足消費者需求。


     (ii)     We or our franchisees may be subject to intellectual property   (ii)    我們或我們的加盟商可能會涉及知識產權侵
              infringement claims or disputes, which may be expensive to              權索賠或糾紛，其可能導致產生高昂的辯護
              defend and may disrupt our business.                                    費用，並可能擾亂我們的業務。


     (iii)    Our store network consists primarily of franchise stores        (iii)   我們的門店網絡主要由加盟商經營的加盟店
              operated by franchisees over whom we have limited control.              組成，而我們對加盟商的控制有限。


     (iv)     We have limited control over franchisees on how they carry      (iv)    我們對加盟商如何與終端消費者進行珠寶產
              out trade-in practice for jewelry products with their end               品以舊換新的控制權有限。
              consumers.


     (v)      Any quality issue related to our products could result in a     (v)     任何與我們產品相關的質量問題都可能導致
              loss of end consumers and sales.                                        失去終端消費者和銷售損失。

LISTING ON HONG KONG STOCK EXCHANGE                                    於香港聯交所上市
On 26 June 2025, the H Shares of the Company were listed                於2025年6月26日，本公司H股於聯交所主板上
on the Main Board of the Stock Exchange. The Global Offering            市，全球發售53,829,200股H股，包括香港公開發
comprised 53,829,200 H Shares, including 23,404,000 H Shares            售 23,404,000 股 H 股 及 國 際 發 售 30,425,200 股 H
under the Hong Kong Public Offering and 30,425,200 H Shares             股。H股乃透過首次公開發售方式按發售價每股H
under the International Offering. The H Shares were issued and          股24.00港元向香港及海外投資者發行及認購。於
subscribed by way of an initial public offering at an offer price of    2025年7月23日，本公司整體協調人悉數行使招股
HK$24.00 per H Share to investors in Hong Kong and overseas.            章程所述的超額配股權，涉及合共8,074,300股H
On 23 July 2025, the overall coordinators of the Company fully          股，按每股H股24.00港元發行及配發,用於補足國
exercised the over-allotment option as stated in the prospectus,        際發售的超額分配。經扣除包銷費用、佣金及其他
involving a total of 8,074,300 H Shares. These H Shares were issued     相關估計上市開支後，全球發售及行使超額配股權
and allotted at HK$24.00 per H Share to cover the over-allotment        的所得款項淨額合共約1,429.48百萬港元。上市所
in relation to the International Offering. The net proceeds from        得款項淨額的擬定及實際用途載列於本年度報告
the global offering and the exercise of the over-allotment option      「管理層討論及分析-上市所得款項淨額用途」                 一節。
amounted to approximately HK$1,429.48 million after deducting
underwriting fees, commissions and other relevant estimated
listing expenses. The proposed and actual use of the net proceeds

     RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL                                      風險管理及內部控制
     The Board acknowledges its responsibility for the risk management         董事會確認其對風險管理及內部控制系統，以及審
     and internal control systems and reviewing their effectiveness.           查其有效性的責任。該等系統旨在管理而非消除未
     Such systems are designed to manage rather than eliminate                 能達成業務目標的風險，而且僅能就防範重大錯報
     the risk of failure to achieve business objectives, and it can only       或損失提供合理而非絕對的保證。
     provide reasonable, but not absolute, assurance in preventing
     material misstatements or losses.


     The Board has overall responsibility for assessing and determining        董事會全面負責評估及釐定本公司達成戰略目標時
     the nature and extent of risks that the Company is willing to             所願意承擔的風險性質及程度，並建立及維護合適
     accept in achieving its strategic objectives, and for establishing        且有效的風險管理及內部控制系統。
     and maintaining appropriate and effective risk management and
     internal control systems.


     The Audit Committee assists the Board in leading management               審計委員會協助董事會領導管理層，並監督其風險
     and overseeing the design, implementation and monitoring of its           管理及內部控制系統的設計、實施及監控。
     risk management and internal control systems.


     Management has reported to the Audit Committee that, at the               日常經營層面我們已構建風險管理及內部控制機制
     day-to-day operational level, we have established three lines of          的三道防線：
     defense for the risk management and internal control mechanism:


     The First Line of Defense comprises the operational business units        第一道防線由各業務部門構成，是風險管理及內部
     and serves as the source and foundation of risk management                控制的源頭與基礎。各業務部門作為風險的直接承
     and internal control. As the direct risk takers, the business units       擔者，負責在日常經營活動中落實風險管理的具
     are responsible for implementing risk management measures in              體執行，將風險管控要求嵌入業務全流程，主動識
     their daily operations, embedding risk control requirements into          別、評估、應對本部門業務範圍內的各類風險，從
     the entire business process, and proactively identifying, assessing       業務前端築牢風險防控的第一道屏障，是整個風控
     and responding to various risks within their respective scopes of         體系落地的核心執行層。
     business. By building the first barrier of risk prevention at the front
     end of business operations, this line constitutes the core execution
     layer for the implementation of the overall risk control system.

of defense, this line is responsible for conducting company-wide          險指引與合規支持，同時對第一道防線的執行有效

professional risk guidance and compliance support to the

independently and objectively supervises, inspects and evaluates          與評價，出具獨立審計意見，向審計委員會、董事

opinions, and reports directly to the Audit Committee and the             公司整體經營的穩健。
Board. It ensures the compliance, effectiveness and independence
of the Company’s risk management and control mechanism,
prevents systemic risks at the top level, and safeguards the overall
stable operation of the Company.

     DIVIDEND POLICY                                                       股息政策
     The Company has implemented a consistent and stable profit            本公司已實行持續、穩定的利潤分配和股息政策。
     distribution and dividend policy.


     In the event that the Company records a profit for the year and       在公司當年盈利且累計未分配利潤為正數，且在滿
     its accumulated undistributed profits are positive, and subject to    足公司正常生產經營的資金需求情況下，如公司外
     meeting the funding requirements for the Company’s normal             部經營環境和經營狀況未發生重大不利變化，公司
     production and operations, provided that there is no material         每年以現金形式分配的股息應當不少於當年實現的
     adverse change in the Company’s external operating environment        可供分配利潤的10%。
     and operating conditions, the dividends to be distributed by the
     Company in cash each year shall not be less than 10% of the
     distributable profits realised in that year.


     On the premise that the net profit attributable to the shareholders   在當年歸屬於公司股東的淨利潤為正的前提下，公
     of the Company for the year is positive, the Company generally        司一般進行年度現金派息。公司董事會也可以根據
     makes annual cash dividend distributions. The Board of the            當期的盈利規模、現金流狀況、發展階段及資金需
     Company may also, based on the scale of profits for the               求狀況，提議公司進行中期或季度現金派息。
     period, cash flow position, stage of development and funding
     requirements, propose that the Company makes an interim or

     In the event that the Company’s operating conditions are sound        在公司經營狀況良好，且董事會認為公司每股收
     and the Board is of the view that the earnings per share and share    益、股票價格與公司股本規模、股本結構不匹配
     price of the Company do not match its share capital size and          時，公司可以在滿足上述現金派息比例的前提下，
     share capital structure, the Company may, subject to satisfying       採取派發股票股息的方式。採用派發股票股息方式
     the above cash dividend distribution ratio, adopt the method of       的，應當具有公司成長性、每股淨資產的攤薄等真

The board of directors of Zhou Liu Fu Jewellery Co., Ltd. (the        周六福珠寶股份有限公司董事會（「董事會」）作為

risk management and information disclosure. The Board is              制定，識別並評估與業務相關的重大ESG風險及機
responsible for overseeing the Company’s ESG direction and            遇，並確保公司建立起行之有效的風險管理及內部

risks and opportunities related to the business, and ensuring that    理與經營決策，維護投資者、消費者、員工、供應
the Company establishes effective risk management and internal        商及社會公眾利益相關方的權益。
control systems. We are committed to integrating the concept

stakeholders, including investors, consumers, employees, suppliers,

      1    PROTECTING THE ENVIRONMENT AND                                      1    保護環境，自然共生
           COEXISTING WITH NATURE
           As a responsible jewelry enterprise, Zhou Liu Fu has                     作為負責任的珠寶首飾企業，周六福建立了
           established a comprehensive environmental management                     完善的環境管理機制，堅守合規底線，實
           mechanism, adheres to the bottom line of compliance,                     現從行政辦公到終端運營的環境管理體系。
           and has implemented an environmental management                          我們持續探索節能減排路徑，優化資源配置
           system that spans from administrative offices to end-point               效率，致力於降低經營活動對周邊生態的影
           operations. We continuously explore pathways for energy                  響，以實際行動築牢綠色運營基石，推動企
           conservation and emission reduction, optimize resource                   業與環境的永續平衡。
           allocation efficiency, and are committed to minimizing
           the impact of our business activities on the surrounding
           ecosystem. Through concrete actions, we strive to
           solidify the foundation of green operations and promote
           a sustainable balance between the enterprise and the
           environment.

                 People ’s Republic of China , to strictly control pollutant              各環節的環境合規性，避免產生負面影
                 emissions and mitigate various environmental impacts.                    響。我們積極制定內部管理制度，明確
                 We ensure environmental compliance at all operational                    公司環境管理標準，推動環保工作高效

    Zhou Liu Fu fully recognizes the strategic impact of         周六福深刻認識到氣候變化對企業長期
    climate change on the Company’s long-term value              價值創造與可持續發展的戰略影響。參
    creation and sustainable development. With reference to      考氣候相關財務披露工作小組(TCFD)框

    (TCFD) framework, we have established a normalized           判，建立了常態化的氣候風險管理機
    climate risk management mechanism through                    制。我們系統性地識別並評估了包括極
    stakeholder communication and expert assessment.             端天氣引發的實體風險，以及政策、法
    We have systematically identified and assessed both          律與市場變革帶來的轉型風險。為構建
    physical risks arising from extreme weather events and       氣候適應型企業，我們針對性地實施了
    transition risks stemming from changes in policies,          一系列緩衝與應對措施，持續強化業務

1.3 Green Operation Practice                                      1.3 綠色運營實踐
    Adhering to the core principle of sustainable development,      周六福秉持可持續發展核心理念，將綠
    Zhou Liu Fu has deeply integrated green operations into         色運營深度融入企業日常運營過程中，
    its daily business processes. We consistently incorporate       我們持續將節能減排、綠色辦公、資源
    practices such as energy conservation and emission              循環等實踐貫穿於各業務環節，力求實
    reduction, eco-friendly office practices, and resource          現企業與自然環境的和諧共生。

 Case Study: Indoor Fresh Air System and Smart Exhaust System for the Parking Garage at
 the Headquarters Complex
 案例：總部基地大樓室內新風系統及停車場智能排氣
 For the Zhou Liu Fu Headquarters Complex currently under construction, we have installed a smart
 ventilation system in both the office building and the underground parking garage to ensure air
 quality and optimize energy efficiency. The office building’s fresh air system is designed to meet high
 standards; it dynamically adjusts the fresh air supply by monitoring CO2 levels, ensuring a healthy
 and comfortable environment while preventing over-ventilation. The underground garage utilizes
 high-precision CO sensors and employs a tiered response mechanism to precisely control the start,
 stop, and speed of the exhaust fans, thereby achieving “demand-based ventilation”. This system
 significantly reduces fan energy consumption through variable frequency control, achieving annual
 energy savings of over 30% and effectively reducing the Company’s operational carbon emissions.
 對於在建的周六福總部大樓，我們在總部辦公樓與地下車庫設置智能通風系統，實現空氣品質保障
 與能源效率優化。辦公樓新風系統依據高標準設計，通過監測CO2濃度動態調節新風量，在保障健
 康舒適環境的同時避免過度通風。地下車庫則採用高精度CO傳感器，設定分級響應機制，精準控
 制排風機的啟停與調速，實現「按需通風」。該系統通過變頻控制顯著降低風機能耗，年節電率可達
 30%以上，有效減少公司運營碳排放。

     Case Study: Implementation of a Fully Intelligent Air Conditioning Control System in the
     Headquarters Complex
     案例：總部基地大樓應用全智能空調控制系統
     We have deployed an intelligent air conditioning control system in the Headquarters Complex. By
     installing temperature and pressure sensors at key points along the chilled water piping, the system
     continuously monitors the temperature difference between the supply and return water as well as
     the pressure differential in the piping network, dynamically adjusting the flow rate of the chilled
     water system to enable intelligent “on-demand cooling” operations.
     我們在總部基地大樓部署智能空調控制系統，通過在冷凍水管路關鍵節點安裝溫度及壓力傳感器，
     實時採集供回水溫差與壓力差，動態調節冷凍水系統流量，實現「按需供冷」智能化運行。

                   Case Study: Construction of a Rainwater Harvesting and Reuse System for the Headquarters
                   Complex
                   案例：總部基地大樓建設雨水收集與回用系統
                   Zhou Liu Fu has implemented a rainwater harvesting and reuse system at its Headquarters Complex
                   to improve water use efficiency. The system efficiently collects rainwater from hard surfaces such
                   as rooftops and roads. After being transported through a dedicated pipeline network, the water
                   undergoes treatment processes including “initial flow diversion” multi-stage filtration, and necessary
                   disinfection to ensure water quality safety. The treated rainwater will be channeled through
                   dedicated green pipes for consistent use in non-potable applications such as landscape irrigation and
                   road washing, thereby enabling the recycling of water resources.
                   周六福在總部基地大廈規劃運行雨水收集與回用系統，旨在提升水資源利用效率。該系統通過屋
                   頂、道路等硬化表面高效收集雨水，經獨立管網輸送後，採用「初期棄流」、多級過濾及必要消毒等
                   處理工藝，確保水質安全。處理後的雨水將通過專用綠色管道，穩定用於綠化灌溉、道路沖洗等非
                   飲用用途，可實現水資源的循環利用。

Case Study: Preserving Intangible Cultural Heritage and Driving Innovation Through Modern
Craftsmanship
案例：非遺文化傳承與現代精工創新
In 2025, the Company launched innovative projects integrating intangible cultural heritage, successfully
introducing the “Jianbao Intangible Cultural Heritage (見寶非遺)” themed art exhibition and the “Pilgrimage
to Jiuhua (尋願九華)” co-branded cultural jewellery series, blending traditional craftsmanship with modern
aesthetics. While preserving traditional craftsmanship and unique characteristics, we utilize modern
technologies such as CNC precision machining to creatively integrate traditional intangible cultural heritage
techniques – including chiseling and enamel painting – with contemporary manufacturing processes. This
has resulted in an innovative product system that embodies cultural significance while meeting modern
aesthetic demands.
2025年公司開展與非遺結合的創新項目，成功推出「見寶非遺」主題藝術展與「尋願九華」聯名文化珠寶系
列，融合傳統工藝與現代審美。在保留傳統工藝與獨特性的同時，我們使用數控精工等現代技術，實現
了非遺鏨刻、琺瑯彩等傳統技藝與當代製造工藝的創造性結合，形成既承載文化內涵又符合現代審美需
求的產品創新體系。

 Case Study: All-Category Integrated Automated Inbound Equipment (Version 2.0)
 案例：2.0版本全品類產品自動化入庫一體設備
 Zhou Liu Fu continues to leverage technology to enhance quality management and is accelerating the
 research, development, and implementation of automated warehousing equipment. At the end of 2024,
 the Company introduced of its transshipment bead integrated automated inbound equipment (version 1.0),
 which includes integrated data analysis capabilities, successfully marking the start of its transition to a high-
 tech operational model. This equipment significantly improves operational accuracy, particularly in terms of
 weighing error control, thereby providing greater assurance of product quality. In 2025, we will advance
 the research, development, and implementation of our all-category integrated automated inbound
 equipment (version 2.0). Currently, the equipment is performing stably and reliably during testing.
 周六福持續推動科技賦能質量管理，加速自動化入庫設備的研發與應用。公司於2024年末引入1.0版本轉
 運珠自動化入庫一體設備，配套實現系統數據分析功能，成功開啟了向高科技作業模式的轉型。該設備
 顯著提升了作業精度，特別是在稱量誤差控制方面表現精準，為產品質量提供了更高保障。2025年，我
 們推進研發並落地2.0版本全品類產品自動化入庫一體設備，目前設備測試表現穩定可靠。

126    Zhou Liu Fu Jewellery Co., Ltd. 周六福珠宝股份有限公司
    ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND GOVERNANCE REPORT
                               環境、社會及管治報告



Remuneration System
薪酬體系
•    Base Pay: We implement a fixed compensation model consisting of a contract-based salary plus
     various allowances, providing employees with a stable income. Salaries are paid on a regular
     basis based on attendance records.
•    基本薪酬：實行合同工資+各項津貼的固定薪酬模式，為員工提供穩定的收入保障。工資依據
     考勤結果，實行定期支付機制。


•    Performance Bonuses: Performance bonuses are established that are closely tied to individual
     and team performance. The Company conducts regular performance evaluations of employees
     in accordance with the Performance Management System . Bonus payments directly reflect
     work performance and contributions, effectively motivating employees to strive for excellence.
•    績效獎金：設立與個人及團隊業績緊密掛鈎的績效獎金。公司依據《績效管理制度》對員工進
     行定期考核，獎金發放直接反映工作成果與貢獻，有效激勵員工追求卓越。

Benefits System
福利體系
•    Comprehensive Leave Policies: We offer a variety of leave options, including seniority leave,
     marriage leave, maternity leave, paternity leave, breastfeeding leave, and bereavement leave,
     to help employees achieve a healthy work-life balance.
•    多元化假期制度：設立司齡假、婚假、產假、陪產假、哺乳假及慰唁假等多元化假期，支持
     員工平衡工作與生活。


•    Allowances and Incentives: We offer perfect attendance bonuses and length-of-service awards,
     and provide housing allowances and meal subsidies based on job position and location,
     demonstrating our genuine care for employees’ daily lives.
•    津貼與獎勵：設立全勤獎、司齡獎，並根據崗位與駐地提供外宿補貼、工作餐補助等，切實
     關懷員工日常生活。


•    Health Management and Activities: We organize free annual health checkups for employees
     and regularly host a variety of cultural activities, such as employee outings, departmental team-
     building events, and monthly birthday celebrations, to promote employees’ physical and mental
     well-being and foster team cohesion.
•    健康管理與活動：每年組織員工免費健康體檢，並定期舉辦員工旅遊、部門團建、月度生日
     會等豐富多彩的文化活動，促進員工身心健康與團隊融合。


•    Holiday Gifts and Family Support: We distribute gifts or cash bonuses during traditional
     holidays such as the Spring Festival, International Women’s Day, the Dragon Boat Festival, and
     the Mid-Autumn Festival, as well as on the Company’s anniversary. We also provide wedding
     and newborn bonuses, and activate our “Care and Support” program for employees’ families
     facing significant hardships, thereby demonstrating the deep bonds of our corporate family.
•    節日禮贈與家庭關懷：於春節、婦女節、端午節、中秋節等傳統節日及公司週年慶發放禮品
     或禮金；提供結婚禮金、新生兒禮金，並對遭遇重大困難的員工家庭啟動「愛心幫扶」機制，
     體現企業大家庭的深厚情誼。

 Case Study: The 15th Top-Performing Employees Dialogue
 案例：第十五期績優員工對話會
 To establish an open and transparent communication mechanism and listen to the voices of our
 younger employees, we successfully held the 15th High-Performing Employees Dialogue in October
 2025. Senior executives of the Company held face-to-face discussions with representatives of
 management trainees on topics such as talent development and cultural heritage. The event featured
 segments such as a talent showcase and a suggestion session, during which management trainees
 offered innovative proposals for the Company’s development from various perspectives. This event
 fostered mutual understanding between the Company and the next generation of talent, providing
 valuable insights for optimizing the training system and supporting strategic development.
 為構建開放透明的溝通機制，傾聽新生代員工心聲，2025年10月，我們成功舉辦第十五期績優員
 工對話會。公司高管與多位管培生代表圍繞人才培養、文化傳承等議題進行面對面交流。會議設有
 風采展示、獻言獻策等環節，管培生員工從多角度為公司發展提出創新建議。本次活動強化了企業
 與新生代人才的雙向理解，為優化培養體系、支撐戰略發展提供了重要參考。

                  其他培訓                  外派學習                    對於因工作需要、經公司批准或指派參加的外部專業
                                                                技術或學位培訓，員工可與公司簽訂《外派培訓協議
                                                                書》，並可按協議約定獲得相應的補貼或費用報銷。

 Case Study: “Strategy Wins the Day, Winning the Future” Zhou Liu Fu Marketing Strategy
 Training Workshop
 案例：「策略制勝，贏戰未來」周六福營銷策略培訓研討班
In August 2025, Zhou Liu Fu hosted the “Strategy Wins the Day, Winning the Future” marketing
strategy training seminar (Phase II of the “Leading Lion” Initiative). The course integrated the wisdom
of The Art of War with practical business applications, systematically covering methods such as
macro-environmental analysis, competitive landscape assessment, marketing strategy formulation,
and action plan design. By blending classical wisdom with modern business practices, the seminar
guided participants to deeply reflect on the essence of strategy.
2025年8月，周六福舉辦「策略制勝，贏戰未來」營銷策略培訓研討班（頭獅工程第二期），課程融合
《孫子兵法》智慧與商業實戰，系統講授宏觀環境分析、競爭形勢研判、營銷策略制定及行動方案設
計等方法，融合經典智慧與現代商業實戰，引導學員深入思考策略本質。




     Figure: “Strategy Wins the Day, Winning the Future” Marketing Strategy Training Workshop
                          圖：「策略制勝，贏戰未來」營銷策略培訓研討班

                   Case Study: Zhou Liu Fu’s 9th “Striving Cup” Skills Competition
                   案例：周六福第九屆「奮鬥杯」技能大賽
                   From July to August 2025, Zhou Liu Fu held the 9th “Striving Cup” Skills Competition, centered on
                   the theme of “Seeking Strivers.” This session of skills competition included 10 training sessions, the
                   distribution of 28 learning materials, and 11 practical skill competitions across various categories. It
                   emphasized using competitions as training and incorporated gamified design to boost participation.
                   Ultimately, the event yielded practical outcomes such as 54 work improvement suggestions and
                   21 process standardization proposals, and recognized 21 outstanding individuals and 3 teams.
                   This competition has created a closed-loop process spanning personal growth and management
                   development, embodying the multifaceted values of being “useful, engaging, and informative,” and
                   has infused the Company with new talent to support its sustained, high-quality growth.
                   2025年7月至8月，周六福開展了以「尋找奮鬥者」為核心的第九屆「奮鬥杯」技能大賽，本屆技能大
                   賽共涵蓋10場培訓、28份學習資料發放及11個賽道實操競賽，強調以賽代訓、遊戲化設計激發參
                   與熱情，最終沉澱54項工作改善建議、21項流程標準化方案等實戰成果，並評選出21名優秀個人
                   與3支團隊。本次大賽實現了從個人成長到管理升級的閉環，體現「有用、有趣、有料」的多元價
                   值，為公司持續高質量發展注入了人才活力。




                                         Figure: Zhou Liu Fu’s 9th “Striving Cup” Skills Competition
                                                    圖：周六福第九屆「奮鬥杯」技能大賽

                        Case: Zhou Liu Fu’s 2nd Employee Badminton Tournament

                        In 2025, in line with its philosophy of “Work Happily, Live Healthily,” Zhou Liu Fu successfully
                        hosted its second employee badminton tournament. The event featured both a recreational
                        challenge and a competitive tournament, combining fun and interaction with professional
                        competition, which effectively sparked everyone’s enthusiasm for sports. This event not
                        only provided employees with an opportunity to relax and unwind, but also significantly
                        strengthened team cohesion and a sense of belonging through physical activity and shared
                        laughter.
                        2025年，為踐行「快樂工作，健康生活」理念，周六福成功舉辦了第二屆社團羽毛球賽。本次
                        活動設置了娛樂挑戰賽與正式競技賽雙賽道，兼顧趣味互動與專業比拼，有效激發了全員運
                        動熱情。該活動不僅為員工提供了放鬆身心的平台，更在揮灑汗水與歡聲笑語中顯著增強了
                        團隊凝聚力與歸屬感。

                        Case Study: “Full Moon at Mid-Autumn, Love at Zhou Liu Fu” Employee Mid-Autumn
                        Festival Event
                        案例：「月滿中秋愛在周六福」員工中秋活動
                        During the 2025 Mid-Autumn Festival, Zhou Liu Fu planned and launched a series of
                        celebratory events titled “Full Moon at Mid-Autumn, Love at Zhou Liu Fu.” By blending
                        traditional charm with modern creativity through a variety of formats, the Company created
                        a festive atmosphere for all employees. Leveraging its “online-offline” integration model, the
                        Company launched an online campaign titled “Three-Line Love Letters: Moonlit Fill-in-the-
                        Blank Notes,” encouraging employees to express their genuine feelings and compiling the best
                        entries into a book. Offline, the Company organized interactive activities such as a traditional
                        Chinese-style concert, a fun mooncake grab, and a riddle wall. The event featured a lively
                        atmosphere and frequent interaction, allowing employees to truly experience the warmth of
                        the holiday season and the Company’s care, while further strengthening team cohesion and a
                        sense of cultural identity.
                        2025年中秋之際，周六福策劃並開展了「月滿中秋愛在周六福」系列慶祝活動，通過融合傳統
                        韻味與現代創意的多元形式，為全體員工營造了濃厚的節日氛圍。公司依託「線上與線下」聯
                        動機制，在線上發起「三行情書 · 月滿填空箋」續寫活動，鼓勵員工抒發真實情感並將優秀作
                        品彙編成冊，在線下則舉辦了國風音樂會、趣味搶月餅及燈謎牆等互動環節。現場氛圍熱烈
                        且互動頻繁，使員工在真切感受節日溫暖與公司關懷的同時，進一步增強了團隊的凝聚力與
                        文化認同感。

Case Study: Supplier Goods Management Training
案例：供應商貨品管理培訓
To standardize supply chain inventory management and mitigate operational risks, the Company organized
specialized training sessions on goods management for its partner suppliers. The sessions focused on key
topics such as labeling standards and requirements for the transshipment and sorting of multiple product
categories, clarifying operational standards and performance evaluation criteria. Through on-site training,
Q&A sessions, and assessments, we have standardized operational procedures for both suppliers and
buyers, effectively improving suppliers’ delivery accuracy and warehouse coordination efficiency, thereby
ensuring the stable operation of the supply chain.
為規範供應鏈貨品管理、降低運營風險，公司組織合作供應商開展貨品管理專項培訓，圍繞貨品貼標規
範、多品類轉運分揀要求等核心內容進行講解，明確操作標準與考核要求。通過現場培訓、答疑與考
核，我們統一供需雙方的操作流程，有效提升供應商貨品交付準確率與倉儲協同效率，為供應鏈穩定運
行提供保障。

              Case Study: Training on Shipping Requirements for Suppliers
              案例：供應商貨品出貨要求培訓
              The Company conducted specialized training for suppliers on shipping requirements, providing detailed
              explanations on net gold labeling standards, shipping verification procedures, and compliance management
              requirements. The training aimed to standardize shipping criteria for both suppliers and the Company,
              strengthen suppliers’ compliance awareness, ensure the quality of delivered goods, and effectively improve
              suppliers’ shipping management capabilities.
              公司面向供應商開展出貨要求專項培訓，聚焦淨金重標註標準、出貨核驗流程及合規管理要求等內容進
              行詳細講解，統一供需雙方的出貨標準，強化供應商的合規意識，保障貨品交付質量，切實提升供應商
              出貨管理水平。

 Case Study: Charity Fundraising to “Help Children with Autism Lead Normal Lives”
 案例：「幫助星星孩子正常生活」愛心募捐
 In November 2025, Zhou Liu Fu joined hands with the Shenzhen Autism Research Association to launch
 the charity fundraising campaign “Help Children with Autism Lead Normal Lives”. Through internal
 mobilization and coordination with retail outlets nationwide, we encouraged employees to share the
 campaign on social media, successfully engaging compassionate individuals from all walks of life.
 Ultimately, we raised RMB17,405.62, all of which was allocated to support the rehabilitation and education
 of children with autism and to update teaching materials.
 2025年11月，周六福攜手深圳市自閉症研究會發起「幫助星星孩子正常生活」愛心募捐。活動通過內部動
 員與全國終端聯動，我們鼓勵員工進行社交分享，成功吸引社會各界愛心力量的共同參與，最終募集善
 款17,405.62元，全部用於自閉症兒童康復助學與教具更新。

              Case Study: “Be Good! Youth” Public Welfare Initiative: Empowering Youth Development
              案例：「向善吧！少年」公益計劃，賦能青少年成長
              Since March 2024, Zhou Liu Fu has been deeply involved in the “Be Good! Youth” public welfare initiative
              initiated by six major departments, including the Civil Affairs Bureau and the Education Bureau of Luohu
              District, to promote the concept of charity on campus. Centered on the goal of “knowing, practicing, and
              promoting kindness,” the project has gradually developed four core initiatives: “Bringing Charity Culture
              into the Classroom,” “Waste Paper Recycling, Benefiting Luohu,” “Small Pockets, Big Hearts,” and “Little
              First-Aid Heroes.” It has also expanded to include distinctive platforms such as the Charity Ecosystem Base,
              the Youth Charity Culture Lecture Series, and the Shenzhen-Hong Kong-Macao Family Smart Reading
              Program. As of 2025, the “Be Good, Youth!” public welfare initiative has reached 28 schools, with more
              than 24,000 student participants and over 120 events held to date.
              自2024年3月起，周六福深度參與由羅湖區民政局、教育局等六大部門發起的「向善吧！少年」公益計
              劃，推動慈善理念走進校園。該項目圍繞「知善、行善、揚善」的目標，逐步形成「慈善文化進課堂」
                                                          「紙等
              你來益動羅湖」

        3.3 Data Privacy Security                                              3.3 數據隱私安全
             Zhou Liu Fu strictly complies with national laws                        周六福嚴格遵守國家網絡安全及數據
             and regulations regarding cybersecurity and data                        保護相關法律法規，制定《信息安全管
             protection. We have established policies such as the                    理制度彙編》
                                                                                          《網絡安全管理制度》等制
             Compilation of Information Security Management                          度，我們堅持「預防為主」的總體方針，
             Systems and the Cybersecurity Management System .                       通過定級備案、安全建設及測評管理，
             Adhering to the overarching principle of “prevention                    確保信息系統全生命週期的合規與穩
             first,” we ensure the compliance and stability of our                   定。公司構建涵蓋物理環境、網絡邊
             information systems throughout their entire lifecycle                   界、主機應用及數據隱私的深度防禦體
             through classification and filing, security infrastructure              系：通過部署防火牆、入侵檢測及多級
             development, and assessment management. The                             加密技術，築牢數據安全屏障；同時實
             Company has established a defense-in-depth system                       施嚴格的訪問控制、身份認證與動態審
             that encompasses the physical environment, network                      計，並配套常態化的安全運維與應急演
             perimeters, host applications, and data privacy: by                     習機制。公司致力於通過制度的持續評
             deploying firewalls, intrusion detection systems,                       審與優化，全面保障核心信息資產的安
             and multi-level encryption technologies, we have                        全性與可用性。
             built a robust data security barrier; at the same
             time, we implement strict access controls, identity
             authentication, and dynamic auditing, complemented
             by routine security operations and emergency response
             drills. The Company is committed to comprehensively
             ensuring the security and availability of its core
             information assets through the continuous review and
             optimization of its policies and procedures.



              Case Study: Cybersecurity Risk Assessment and Targeted Hardening
              案例：網絡安全風險評估與專項加固
              In 2025, Zhou Liu Fu conducted in-depth vulnerability scans and risk assessments on more than 200
              authorized servers and devices. Through a technical assessment, the Company identified assets and
              exploitable vulnerabilities across high, medium, and low risk levels, and submitted detailed security
              hardening recommendations based on the assessment report. In response to the identified security risks,
              Zhou Liu Fu strictly implemented its remediation plan, prioritizing the resolution of high-risk vulnerabilities.
              Through timely patch deployment and system optimization, the Company effectively eliminated potential
              threats and further strengthened its overall cybersecurity defense system.
              2025年，周六福針對200餘台授權主機及設備開展了深度漏洞掃描與風險評估。通過技術摸排，公司識
              別出涉及高、中、低不同風險等級的資產及可利用漏洞，並根據評估報告提交了詳盡的安全加固建議。
              針對識別出的安全隱患，周六福嚴格落實整改方案，優先修復高風險漏洞，通過及時的補丁部署與系統
              優化，有效消除了潛在威脅，進一步夯實了整體網絡安全防禦體系。

 Case: A Practical Network and Data Security Attack-Defense Drill
 案例：網絡與數據安全實戰攻防演練
 In December 2025, the Company participated in a practical network and data security attack-defense
 drill organised by the Cyberspace Administration of Luohu District. The training covered key areas such as
 account and password protection, sensitive data protection and the strengthening of weak passwords,
 covering all second-level subdomain systems under the Company’s domain name. Following the drill, the
 Company implemented security measures such as mandatory use of strong passwords and upgrades to
 login verification mechanisms. All application systems underwent security scans to enhance their protective
 capabilities.
 2025年12月，公司參與羅湖區網信辦組織的網絡與數據安全實戰攻防演練，培訓涵蓋賬戶密碼防護、敏
 感數據保護、弱口令加固等關鍵領域，覆蓋公司域名下全部二級子域名系統。演練後，公司實施強密碼
 強制使用、登錄驗證機制升級等安全措施，所有應用系統均通過安全掃描檢測，提升防護能力。

      Part D: Climate-related Disclosures                                         D部分：氣候相關披露

                                                                                                                      Disclosure Location
       Climate-related Disclosures                                                                                    or Explanatory Notes
       氣候相關披露                                                                                                         披露位置或解釋
       (I) Governance
       (I)管治
       19.               (a)   The governance body(s) (which can include a board, committee or equivalent             Board Statement
                               body charged with governance) or individual(s) responsible for oversight of            Respond to Climate
                               climate-related risks and opportunities.                                               Change
                         (a)   負責監督氣候相關風險和機遇的治理機構（可包括董事會、委員會或其他同等                                                     ESG Governance
                               治理機構）或個人的資訊。                                                                           董事會聲明

                               to monitor, manage and oversee climate-related risks and opportunities.                ESG管治

                        targets the issuer is required to meet by law or regulation, including any greenhouse       應對氣候變化
                        gas emissions targets.
                        發行人須披露(a)其為監察實現其策略目標的進展而設定的與氣候相關的定性及量化目
                        標；及(b)法律或法規要求發行人達到的任何目標，包括任何溫室氣體排放目標。
      38.               An issuer shall disclose information about its approach to setting and reviewing each
                        target, and how it monitors progress against each target.
                        發行人須披露其設定及審核每項目標的方法，以及其如何監察達標進度。
      39.               An issuer shall disclose information about its performance against each climate-related
                        target and an analysis of trends or changes in the issuer’s performance.
                        發行人須披露有關每項氣候相關目標的績效的資訊以及對發行人績效的趨勢或變化分
                        析。

         is an entity (including a structured entity), directly            實體）。當本集團對參與投資對象業務
         or indirectly, controlled by the Company. Control is              所得的浮動回報承擔風險或享有權利以
         achieved when the Group is exposed, or has rights, to             及能透過對投資對象的權力（即本集團

         and has the ability to affect those returns through its           動的既存權利）影響該等回報時，即取
         power over the investee (i.e., existing rights that give          得控制權。
         the Group the current ability to direct the relevant
         activities of the investee).


         Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of              一般而言，假設大多數投票權導致控
         voting rights results in control. When the Company                制。倘本公司擁有少於投資對象大多數
         has less than a majority of the voting or similar rights          投票或類似權利的權利，則本集團於評
         of an investee, the Group considers all relevant facts            估其是否擁有對投資對象的權力時會考
         and circumstances in assessing whether it has power               慮一切相關事實及情況，包括：
         over an investee, including:

2.   ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)                                 2.   會計政策（續）
     2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                     2.4 重大會計政策（續）
         (Continued)
        Fair value measurement                                              公允價值計量

        The Group measures its equity investments at fair                   本集團於年度結束時按公允價值計量其
        value at the end of the year. Fair value is the price that          股權投資。公允價值為市場參與者於計
        would be received to sell an asset or paid to transfer              量日期進行的有序交易中出售資產所收
        a liability in an orderly transaction between market                取或轉讓負債所支付的價格。公允價值
        participants at the measurement date. The fair value                計量是基於假設出售資產或轉讓負債的
        measurement is based on the presumption that the                    交易於資產或負債的主要市場，或於無
        transaction to sell the asset or transfer the liability             主要市場的情況下，在對資產或負債的
        takes place either in the principal market for the asset            最有利市場進行。本集團能夠進入主要
        or liability, or in the absence of a principal market, in           或最有利市場。資產或負債的公允價值

        The principal or the most advantageous market must                  用的假設計量（假設市場參與者依照彼

        or a liability is measured using the assumptions that
        market participants would use when pricing the asset

                 The Group uses valuation techniques that are                         本集團使用適用於其情況的估值方法，

                 maximising the use of relevant observable inputs and                 不可觀察輸入值。
                 minimising the use of unobservable inputs.

                            liabilities

                            the lowest level input that is significant to                  言屬重大的直接或間接可觀
                            the fair value measurement is observable,                      察最低級輸入值的估值方法
                            either directly or indirectly


                 Level 3 – based on valuation techniques for which                    第三級 － 根據採用對公允價值計量而
                            the lowest level input that is significant to                  言屬重大的不可觀察最低級
                            the fair value measurement is unobservable                     輸入值的估值方法

                 For assets and liabilities that are recognised in the                就按經常性基準於財務報表確認的資產
                 financial statements on a recurring basis, the Group                 及負債而言，本集團於各報告期末透過
                 determines whether transfers have occurred between                   重新評估分類釐定轉撥是否於各級之間

        from other assets or groups of assets, in which case                 定。
        the recoverable amount is determined for the cash-

        the smallest group of cash-generating units.

        discount rate that reflects current market assessments               貼現率折現至其現值。減值虧損於其產
        of the time value of money and the risks specific to the             生的期間在損益中支銷，並計入與減值
        asset. An impairment loss is charged to the statement                資產的功能一致的有關費用類別內。
        of profit or loss in the period in which it arises in those

        The Group’s business model for managing financial                 本集團管理金融資產的業務模式指其管

        Derecognition of financial assets                                   終止確認金融資產

        A financial asset (or, where applicable, a part of a                金融資產（或（如適用）一項金融資產的
        financial asset or part of a group of similar financial             一部分或一組類似金融資產的一部分）
        assets) is primarily derecognised (i.e., removed from               將初步在下列情況下終止確認（即從本
        the Group’s consolidated statement of financial                     集團綜合財務狀況表移除）：
        position) when:


        •     the rights to receive cash flows from the asset               •   收取資產所得現金流量的權利屆
              have expired; or                                                  滿；或


        •     the Group has transferred its rights to receive               •   本集團轉讓收取資產所得現金流
              cash flows from the asset or has assumed an                       量的權利，或已根據一項「轉付」
              obligation to pay the received cash flows in full                 安排承擔責任，在並無重大延誤
              without material delay to a third party under a                   的情況下，將所得現金流量全數
              “pass-through” arrangement; and either (a) the                    付予第三方；且(a)本集團已轉讓
              Group has transferred substantially all the risks                 資產的絕大部分風險及回報；或
              and rewards of the asset, or (b) the Group has                    (b)本集團並無轉讓或保留資產的
              neither transferred nor retained substantially                    絕大部分風險及回報，但已轉讓
              all the risks and rewards of the asset, but has                   資產的控制權。
              transferred control of the asset.


        When the Group has transferred its rights to receive                倘本集團轉讓收取資產所得現金流量的
        cash flows from an asset or has entered into a                      權利或已訂立轉付安排，則本集團會評
        pass-through arrangement, it evaluates if, and to                   估有否保留該資產所有權的風險及回報
        what extent, it has retained the risk and rewards of                以及其程度。於並無轉讓或保留資產的
        ownership of the asset. When it has neither transferred             絕大部分風險及回報，亦無轉讓資產控
        nor retained substantially all the risks and rewards                制權時，本集團就持續參與已轉讓資產
        of the asset nor transferred control of the asset, the              的程度持續確認有關資產。在此情況
        Group continues to recognise the transferred asset                  下，本集團亦確認相關負債。已轉讓資
        to the extent of the Group’s continuing involvement.                產及相關負債按反映本集團所保留權利

        liability. The transferred asset and the associated

      2.    ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)                                   2.   會計政策（續）
            2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                       2.4 重大會計政策（續）
                (Continued)
                 Impairment of financial assets                                      金融資產減值

                 The Group recognises an allowance for expected                      本集團就所有並非以公允價值計量且其
                 credit losses (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held            變動計入當期損益的債務工具確認預期
                 at fair value through profit or loss. ECLs are based on             信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）撥備。預期
                 the difference between the contractual cash flows                   信貸虧損基於根據合約到期的合約現金
                 due in accordance with the contract and all the cash                流量與本集團預期收取的所有現金流量
                 flows that the Group expects to receive, discounted                 之間的差額而計算，並以原實際利率的
                 at an approximation of the original effective interest              近似值貼現。預期現金流量將包括出售
                 rate. The expected cash flows will include cash                     所持抵押品的現金流量或組成合約條款
                 flows from the sale of collateral held or other credit              的其他信貸提升措施。
                 enhancements that are integral to the contractual
                 terms.


                 General approach                                                    一般方法

                 ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit                       預期信貸虧損分兩階段確認。就初始確
                 exposures for which there has not been a significant                認以來信貸風險並無大幅增加的信貸敞
                 increase in credit risk since initial recognition, ECLs             口而言，會為未來12個月可能發生的
                 are provided for credit losses that result from default             違約事件所產生的信貸虧損（12個月預
                 events that are possible within the next 12 months (a               期信貸虧損）計提預期信貸虧損撥備。
                 12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for which                 就初始確認以來信貸風險大幅增加的信
                 there has been a significant increase in credit risk since          貸敞口而言，須就預期於敞口的餘下年
                 initial recognition, a loss allowance is required for               期產生的信貸虧損計提虧損撥備（不論
                 credit losses expected over the remaining life of the               違約的時間）
                                                                                          （全期預期信貸虧損）。
                 exposure, irrespective of the timing of the default (a
                 lifetime ECL).


                 At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether                  本集團於各個報告日期評估金融工具的
                 the credit risk on a financial instrument has increased             信貸風險是否自初始確認以來顯著增
                 significantly since initial recognition. When making                加。於評估時，本集團將於報告日期金
                 the assessment, the Group compares the risk of a                    融工具發生違約的風險與初始確認日期
                 default occurring on the financial instrument as at                 金融工具發生違約的風險進行比較，並
                 the reporting date with the risk of a default occurring             考慮了無需不必要成本或精力即可取得
                 on the financial instrument as at the date of initial               的合理及可靠資料，包括歷史及前瞻性
                 recognition and considers reasonable and supportable                資料。本集團將逾期30日以上的合約
                 information that is available without undue cost                    付款視為信貸風險顯著增加。
                 or effort, including historical and forward-looking
                 information. The Group considers that there has been
                 a significant increase in credit risk when contractual
                 payments are more than 30 days past due.

                       differences will reverse in the foreseeable future                 稅利潤時確認遞延稅項資產。
                       and taxable profit will be available against which
                       the temporary differences can be utilised.


                 The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed               於各報告期末審閱遞延稅項資產的賬面
                 at the end of each reporting period and reduced to                   值，如不再可能有足夠的應課稅利潤以

                 the deferred tax asset to be utilised. Unrecognised                  確認的遞延稅項資產，倘可能有足夠的

                 Other employee benefits                                            其他僱員福利

                 Pension schemes                                                    退休金計劃

                 The employees of the Group’ subsidiaries which                     本集團主要於中國內地營運的附屬公司
                 mainly operate in Chinese mainland are required to                 的僱員須參與由當地市政府設立的中央
                 participate in a central pension scheme operated by                退休金計劃。該等附屬公司須按其工資
                 the local municipal government. These subsidiaries                 成本若干比例向中央退休金計劃供款。
                 are required to contribute a certain percentage of                 根據中央退休金計劃規則，有關供款將

                 contributions are charged to the statement of profit
                 or loss as they become payable in accordance with the
                 rules of the central pension scheme.

            amount outstanding, the structured deposit is measured at                 （第三級）。

    OBJECTIVES AND POLICIES
   The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise                 本集團的主要金融工具包括計息銀行借款、
   interest-bearing bank borrowings, restricted deposits and            受限制存款以及現金及現金等價物。該等金
   cash and cash equivalents. The main purpose of these                 融工具主要用於為本集團的營運籌集資金。
   financial instruments is to raise finance for the Group’s            本集團有多項其他金融資產及負債，例如直
   operations. The Group has various other financial assets and         接自經營業務產生的貿易及其他應收款項以
   liabilities such as trade and other receivables and trade and        及貿易及其他應付款項。
   other payables, which arise directly from its operations.


   The main risks arising from the Group’s financial instruments        本集團金融工具所產生的主要風險為信用風
   are credit risk and liquidity risk. The board of directors           險及流動性風險。董事會審閱並同意管理此
   reviews and agrees policies for managing each of these risks         等風險的政策，該等政策概述如下。
   and they are summarised below.


   Credit risk                                                          信用風險
   The Group trades only with recognised and creditworthy               本集團只與公認的、信譽良好的第三方進行
   third parties. It is the Group’s policy that all customers who       交易。本集團的政策規定，所有希望以信
   wish to trade on credit terms are subject to credit verification     用條款進行交易的客戶均需接受信用核查程
   procedures. There are no significant concentrations of credit        序。由於本集團的客戶群分散，故來自第三
   risk for trade receivables from third parties as the customer        方的貿易應收款項並無重大集中信用風險。
   bases of the Group are dispersed. In addition, receivable            此外，本集團持續監控應收款項結餘。
   balances are monitored on an ongoing basis.

         maturity of both its financial instruments and financial assets                和金融資產（例如，貿易應收款項和經營活動
         (e.g., trade receivables) and projected cash flows from                        產生的預計現金流量）的到期日。
         operations.

         between the consistency and flexibility of financing activities.

      40. EVENT AFTER THE RELEVANT PERIODS                                   40. 有關期間後事項
         On 16 January 2026, the first extraordinary general meeting           2026年1月16日，本公司第一次臨時股東大
         of the Company (the “2026 First EGM”) considered and                  會（「2026年第一次臨時股東大會」）審議批准
         approved the grant of general mandate to the Board to                 授予董事會回購H股的一般授權。回購的H股
         repurchase H Shares. The amount of H Shares repurchased               數量不得超過截至2026年第一次臨時股東大
         shall not exceed 10% of the total number of H Shares in issue         會當日已發行H股總數的10%。
